   16: =head1 NAME
   17: 
   18: HTML::D3 - A simple Perl module for generating charts using D3.js.
   19: 
   20: =head1 VERSION
   21: 
   22: Version 0.27
   23: 
   24: =cut
   25: 
   26: our $VERSION = '0.27';
   27: 
   28: =head1 SYNOPSIS
   29: 
   30:     use HTML::D3;
   31: 
   32:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(
   33:         width => 1024,
   34:         height => 768,
   35:         title => 'Sample Bar Chart'
   36:     );
   37: 
   38:     my $data = [
   39:         ['Category 1', 10],
   40:         ['Category 2', 20],
   41:         ['Category 3', 30]
   42:     ];
   43: 
   44:     my $html = $chart->render_bar_chart($data);
   45:     print $html;
   46: 
   47:     $chart = HTML::D3->new(title => 'Sales Data');
   48: 
   49:     $data = [
   50:         ['Product A', 100],
   51:         ['Product B', 150],
   52:         ['Product C', 200]
   53:     ];
   54: 
   55:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart($data);
   56:     print $html;
   57: 
   58: =head1 DESCRIPTION
   59: 
   60: HTML::D3 is a Perl module that provides functionality to create simple charts using D3.js.
   61: The module generates HTML and JavaScript code to render the chart in a web browser.
   62: 
   63: Text in a chart's data and options is written into a C<E<lt>scriptE<gt>> so that it
   64: cannot end the script: C<E<lt>> is written as C<\u003c>, which JavaScript reads back
   65: as the same character.  An C<id> is written into an attribute, a selector and a
   66: script, so it must be letters, digits, C<_> and C<->, not starting with a digit or
   67: C<->.  Any other C<id> is ignored and the method's default used, so read the id a
   68: method actually used from the C<svg_id> or C<table_id> it returns.
   69: 
   70: =head1 METHODS
   71: 
   72: The C<=head3 API SPECIFICATION> subsections use L<Params::Validate::Strict>
   73: schema syntax (C<< type => 'arrayref' >> etc.) as a documentation convention.
   74: The module is also used at runtime in C<new()> to validate constructor
   75: arguments; it is therefore a required runtime dependency.  The schemas describe
   76: the parameter contract in machine-readable notation and can be plumbed into a
   77: WAF or test generator if desired.
   78: 
   79: =head2 new
   80: 
   81:     my $chart = HTML::D3->new(%args);
   82: 
   83: Creates a new HTML::D3 object.
   84: Accepts the following optional arguments:
   85: 
   86: =over 4
   87: 
   88: =item * C<width> - The width of the chart (default: 800).
   89: 
   90: =item * C<height> - The height of the chart (default: 600).
   91: 
   92: =item * C<title> - The title of the chart (default: 'Chart').
   93: It is plain text, escaped when it is written to the page: C<E<lt>> and C<&> appear
   94: as themselves, and HTML in a title is shown rather than obeyed.
   95: 
   96: =item * C<responsive> - If true, SVG elements are rendered with
   97: C<viewBox> and C<width="100%" height="auto"> instead of fixed pixel
   98: dimensions, so the chart scales fluidly with its container.
   99: For snippet methods the per-call C<< opts => { responsive => 1 } >>
  100: takes precedence over this object-level setting (default: 0).
  101: 
  102: =back
  103: 
  104: =cut
  105:

  154: # The categorical colour schemes, written down once.
  155: #
  156: # The scheme names and the colours themselves live here and nowhere else.  The
  157: # snippets inline these colours as a JavaScript array - not as a d3.scheme*
  158: # expression - so a page draws the exact numbers palette() hands a map, and a
  159: # change to d3's own palette cannot move a chart's colours under it.  Perl
  160: # callers - a map, a legend, a static renderer - read the same colours through
  161: # categorical_schemes(), palette() and colour_at().  A chart and a map of the
  162: # same data therefore cannot disagree about what scheme 'set2' means, which two
  163: # separate lists of names and colours eventually would.
  164: #
  165: # The colours are D3's own, in D3's order: the scheme arrays in
  166: # d3-scale-chromatic 3.x, which is the version d3 v7 - what _head() loads -
  167: # bundles.  Each entry names the d3 scheme it transcribes, so the palette can be
  168: # checked against the library (t/d3-schemes.t does that when Node and d3 are
  169: # installed), but the chart does not depend on d3 for the numbers it draws.
  170: my %CATEGORICAL_SCHEMES = (
  171: 	tableau10 => {
  172: 		colours => [ '#4e79a7', '#f28e2c', '#e15759', '#76b7b2', '#59a14f',

  191: # The scheme a chart draws with when it is not told which one to use.
  192: my $DEFAULT_CATEGORICAL_SCHEME = 'tableau10';
  193: 
  194: # The palette the charts that offer no scheme option draw with: the pie family
  195: # and the multi-series line charts that predate the option have always drawn
  196: # category10.  The colours are read from the definition above rather than
  197: # written out again here, so that a chart with no choice and a chart told which
  198: # scheme to use are the same palette and not two copies of it.
  199: my $CATEGORY10_JS = _scheme_colours_js('category10');
  200: 
  201: =head2 categorical_schemes
  202: 
  203:     my $names = HTML::D3->categorical_schemes();
  204: 
  205: Returns an arrayref of the names of the categorical colour schemes this module
  206: can draw with, sorted alphabetically: C<category10>, C<paired>, C<set2>,
  207: C<set3> and C<tableau10>.
  208: These are the names the C<scheme> and C<color_scheme> options accept.
  209: 
  210: =cut
  211: 
  212: sub categorical_schemes
  213: {
  214: 	return [ sort keys %CATEGORICAL_SCHEMES ];
  215: }
  216: 
  217: =head2 palette
  218: 
  219:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette('set2');
  220: 
  221: Returns an arrayref of the colours of one scheme - the same colours, in the same
  222: order, that a chart drawn with C<< scheme => 'set2' >> draws with.
  223: A map, a legend or a static renderer can therefore colour the same data the way
  224: the chart does, from Perl, without a browser:
  225: 
  226:     my $scheme  = 'set2';
  227:     my $colours = HTML::D3->palette($scheme);
  228:     my $index   = 0;
  229:     for my $category (@categories) {
  230:         printf "%s is %s\n", $category, HTML::D3->colour_at($scheme, $index++);
  231:     }
  232: 
  233: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for a name that is not in
  234: C<categorical_schemes()>, rather than quietly returning the default scheme's
  235: colours, so that a misspelt scheme is found where it is asked for.
  236: The arrayref returned is a copy: pushing onto it does not change the palette.
  237: 
  238: =head2 colour_at
  239: 
  240:     my $fill = HTML::D3->colour_at('tableau10', $index);
  241: 
  242: Returns the colour one scheme gives the C<$index>th category, counting from
  243: zero, cyclically: an index past the end of the palette wraps around to its
  244: start, which is how C<d3.scaleOrdinal> - what the snippets draw with - assigns
  245: colours.
  246: Colour 0 is the first colour of the scheme, the one C<render_histogram_snippet>
  247: uses as the bar fill when it is given a C<scheme>.
  248: 
  249: The British spelling matches the C<colour_by_series> option.
  250: Dies with C<Unknown scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for an unknown scheme, and with
  251: C<Colour index must be a non-negative integer> for an index that is not one.
  252: 
  253: =cut
  254:

  261: 	});

  263: }

  571: sub render_line_chart {
  572: 	my $self = shift;
  573: 	my $raw  = Params::Get::get_params('data', \@_) // {};
  574: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($raw->{data}) eq 'ARRAY';
  575: 	Params::Validate::Strict::validate_strict({
  576: 		args   => $raw,
  577: 		schema => { data => { type => 'arrayref', error_msg => 'Data must be an array of arrays' } },
  578: 		unknown_parameter_handler => 'ignore',
  579: 	});
  580: 	my $data = $raw->{data};
  581: 
  582: 	# Generate JSON for data
  583: 	my $json_data = _script_json([
  584: 		map { { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] } } @$data
  585: 	]);
  586: 
  587: 	# Generate HTML and D3.js code
  588: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
  589: 	$html .= $self->_head();
  590: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
  591: <body>
  592:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">@{[ $self->_title_html() ]}</h1>
  593:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
  594:     <script>
  595: 	const data = $json_data;
  596: 
  597: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
  598: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
  599: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
  600: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  601: 
  602: 	const x = d3.scalePoint()
  603: 	    .domain(data.map(d => d.label))
  604: 	    .range([0, width]);
  605: 
  606: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
  607: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data, d => d.value)])
  608: 	    .nice()
  609: 	    .range([height, 0]);
  610: 
  611: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
  612: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
  613: 
  614: 	// Draw line
  615: 	const line = d3.line()
  616: 	    .x(d => x(d.label))
  617: 	    .y(d => y(d.value));
  618: 
  619: 	chart.append("path")
  620: 	    .datum(data)
  621: 	    .attr("fill", "none")
  622: 	    .attr("stroke", "steelblue")
  623: 	    .attr("stroke-width", 2)
  624: 	    .attr("d", line);
  625: 
  626: 	// Add points to the line
  627: 	chart.selectAll("circle")
  628: 	    .data(data)
  629: 	    .join("circle")
  630: 	    .attr("cx", d => x(d.label))
  631: 	    .attr("cy", d => y(d.value))
  632: 	    .attr("r", 4)
  633: 	    .attr("fill", "steelblue");
  634: 
  635: 	// Add axes
  636: 	chart.append("g")
  637: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
  638: 
  639: 	chart.append("g")
  640: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
  641: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
  642: 	    .selectAll("text")
  643: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
  644: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
  645:     </script>
  646: </body>
  647: </html>
  648: HTML
  649: 
  650: 	return $html;

  753: 	path

  772: 	    .duration(300)
  773: 	    .attr("opacity", 1);
  774: 
  775: 	chart.append("g")

  777: 
  778: 	chart.append("g")
  779: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)

  792: =head2 render_pie_chart
  793: 
  794:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data);
  795:     my $html = $chart->render_pie_chart($data, { separator => ':' });
  796: 
  797: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a pie chart.
  798: Each slice is coloured with the ten hex values of C<d3.schemeCategory10>,
  799: inlined into the page rather than read from D3's own palette (see L</palette>);
  800: percentage labels appear inside each slice and a colour legend is shown to the
  801: right of the pie.
  802: Accepts the following arguments:
  803: 
  804: =over 4
  805: 
  806: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each data point is an
  807: array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
  808: 
  809: =item * C<%opts> - Optional hashref of options.
  810: 
  811: =over 8
  812: 
  813: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
  814: label and value in the SVG legend.
  815: 
  816: =back
  817: 
  818: =back
  819: 
  820: Returns a string containing the complete HTML5 document.
  821: 
  822: =head3 Errors
  823: 
  824: =over 4
  825: 
  826: =item * Throws C<Data is not optional> when C<$data> is C<undef>.
  827: 
  828: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
  829: 
  830: =back
  831: 
  832: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
  833: 
  834: =head4 Input
  835: 
  836:     {
  837:         data => {
  838: 		type => 'arrayref',
  839: 		element_type => [ 'string', 'number' ]
  840: 	},
  841:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
  842:     }
  843: 
  844:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
  845:     non-arrayref dies.
  846:     Recognised C<opts> key: C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>)
  847:     - character shown between label and value in the SVG legend.
  848: 
  849: =head4 Output
  850: 
  851:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; pie rendered with C<d3.pie()> and
  852:            C<d3.arc()>; slices coloured with category10's hex values,
  853:            inlined; percentage label inside each slice; legend to the right.
  854: 
  855: =cut

 1135: =head2 render_pie_chart_snippet
 1136: 
 1137:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices);
 1138:     my $fragment = $chart->render_pie_chart_snippet(\@slices, \%opts);
 1139:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'pie_chart'
 1140:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1141: 
 1142: Generates an embeddable pie or donut chart fragment for use in existing HTML
 1143: layouts.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'pie_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1144: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1145: 
 1146: =head3 Data format
 1147: 
 1148: Each element of C<\@slices> is C<[$label, $value]> or C<[$label, $value, \%extra]>.
 1149: Negative values are silently converted to their absolute value.  Zero-value
 1150: slices are silently omitted.  C<\%extra> key/value pairs are shown as
 1151: additional rows in the hover tooltip.
 1152: 
 1153: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1154: 
 1155: =over 4
 1156: 
 1157: =item * C<animated> (bool, default 0) - fan slices in from arc-length 0 on
 1158: first render using C<attrTween> / C<d3.easeBackOut> (800 ms, staggered).
 1159: Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 1160: 
 1161: =item * C<donut> (bool, default 0) - render as a donut chart (inner radius
 1162: 38% of outer radius); the total sum appears in the centre hole.
 1163: 
 1164: =item * C<sort_slices> (string, default C<'none'>) - C<'value'> for
 1165: largest-first, C<'label'> for alphabetical, C<'none'> for input order.
 1166: 
 1167: =item * C<max_slices> (int, default 0) - when E<gt> 0, only the top N-1
 1168: slices are shown individually; the rest are collapsed into an C<"Other"> slice.
 1169: 
 1170: =item * C<legend> (bool, default 1) - render an HTML legend panel beside the chart.
 1171: 
 1172: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical
 1173: colour scheme.  Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>,
 1174: C<paired> - the names C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()>
 1175: makes available to Perl.
 1176: 
 1177: =item * C<separator> (string, default C<'/'>) - Character shown between the
 1178: label and value in each legend entry (e.g. C<'/'> produces
 1179: C<Label / 12.34 (42.0%)>, C<':'> produces C<Label : 12.34 (42.0%)>).
 1180: 
 1181: =back
 1182: 
 1183: =head3 Errors
 1184: 
 1185: =over 4
 1186: 
 1187: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<\@slices> is not an ARRAY reference.
 1188: 
 1189: =back
 1190:

 1198:     }

 1461: =head2 render_heatmap_snippet
 1462: 
 1463:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples);
 1464:     my $fragment = $chart->render_heatmap_snippet(\@triples, \%opts);
 1465:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - always 'heatmap'
 1466:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable fragment; caller must load D3 v7
 1467: 
 1468: Generates an embeddable grid heatmap for use in existing HTML layouts.
 1469: Each cell sits at the intersection of an X-axis label and a Y-axis label;
 1470: its colour encodes the cell's numeric value using a sequential D3 colour
 1471: scale.  Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'heatmap', html =E<gt> Str }>.  The
 1472: caller is responsible for loading D3 v7 before embedding the fragment.
 1473: 
 1474: =head3 Data format
 1475: 
 1476: Each element of C<\@triples> is C<[$x_label, $y_label, $value]>.
 1477: C<$value> must be numeric or C<undef> (C<undef> rows are silently
 1478: skipped).  Zero is a valid value and maps to the lightest cell colour.
 1479: The caller is responsible for any aggregation: if multiple triples share
 1480: the same (x_label, y_label) pair, the last one wins.
 1481: 
 1482: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1483: 
 1484: =over 4
 1485: 
 1486: =item * C<color_scheme> (string, default C<'YlOrRd'>) - D3 sequential
 1487: colour scheme.  Supported: C<YlOrRd>, C<Blues>, C<Greens>, C<Purples>,
 1488: C<RdPu>, C<YlGnBu>.
 1489: 
 1490: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title below the X axis.
 1491: The value is JavaScript-escaped (backslash, double-quote, newline, carriage
 1492: return, C<E<lt>>) before being embedded in the page; other characters are taken
 1493: literally.
 1494: 
 1495: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Axis title left of the Y axis.
 1496: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1497: 
 1498: =item * C<val_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Tooltip value label.
 1499: Same JS-escaping as C<x_label> applies.
 1500: 
 1501: =item * C<show_values> (bool, default 0) - Print value inside each cell.
 1502: Auto-suppressed when any cell is narrower than 28 px.
 1503: 
 1504: =item * C<cell_padding> (int 0-8, default 2) - Gap in pixels between cells.
 1505: 
 1506: =item * C<legend> (bool, default 1) - Render a colour-scale legend bar.
 1507: 
 1508: =item * C<animated> (bool, default 0) - Fade cells in on first load.
 1509: Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 1510: 
 1511: =back
 1512: 
 1513: =head3 Errors
 1514: 
 1515: =over 4
 1516: 
 1517: =item * Dies with C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<\@triples>
 1518: is not an ARRAY reference.
 1519: 
 1520: =item * Dies with C<Each data point must be an array reference> when a
 1521: triple element is not an arrayref.
 1522: 
 1523: =item * Dies with C<Each data point must have at least 3 elements> when
 1524: a triple has fewer than 3 elements.
 1525: 
 1526: =item * Dies with C<Value must be numeric> when C<$value> is defined but
 1527: not numeric.
 1528: 
 1529: =item * Dies with C<Unknown color_scheme: E<lt>nameE<gt>> for an
 1530: unsupported C<color_scheme> value.
 1531: 
 1532: =item * Dies with C<cell_padding must be between 0 and 8> when
 1533: C<cell_padding> is outside the valid range.
 1534: 
 1535: =back
 1536: 
 1537: =head3 Side Effects
 1538: 
 1539: Appends a tooltip C<div> to the page when the fragment is rendered in
 1540: the browser.
 1541: 
 1542: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1543: 
 1544: =head4 Input
 1545: 
 1546:     {
 1547:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },

 1549:     }
 1550: 
 1551:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Str, Num|undef ]>.

 1553:     C<x_label> (string, default C<''>), C<y_label> (string, default C<''>),
 1554:     C<val_label> (string, default C<'Value'>), C<show_values> (boolean,

 1556:     C<legend> (boolean, default C<1>), C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>).

 1808: 
 1809: =head2 render_bar_chart_snippet
 1810: 
 1811:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars);
 1812:     my $result = $chart->render_bar_chart_snippet(\@bars, \%opts);
 1813: 
 1814: Generates an embeddable D3.js v7 bar chart fragment.  Returns a hashref
 1815: C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'bar_chart', html =E<gt> $str }> where C<$str> is a
 1816: self-contained HTML fragment (no page shell, no D3 CDN tag) that the caller
 1817: embeds directly after loading D3.js.  C<$str> is a Perl character string
 1818: with the UTF-8 flag set (or pure ASCII when all labels are ASCII).
 1819: 
 1820: Each element of C<\@bars> is an array reference:
 1821: 
 1822:     [ $label, $value ]
 1823:     [ $label, $value, \%extra ]
 1824: 
 1825: C<$label> is the category name (string); C<$value> is a non-negative number
 1826: (negative values are silently converted to their absolute value); the optional
 1827: C<\%extra> hashref supplies additional key/value pairs shown in the hover
 1828: tooltip.  Data points with an undefined C<$value> are silently skipped.
 1829: 
 1830: =head3 Options (C<\%opts>)
 1831: 
 1832: =over 4
 1833: 
 1834: =item C<orientation> (string, default C<'vertical'>)
 1835: 
 1836: C<'vertical'> draws bars rising from a horizontal category axis.
 1837: C<'horizontal'> draws bars extending from a vertical category axis.
 1838: 
 1839: =item C<sort_bars> (string, default C<'none'>)
 1840: 
 1841: Sort order applied before rendering: C<'value'> sorts descending by bar
 1842: height, C<'label'> sorts ascending alphabetically, C<'none'> preserves the
 1843: input order.
 1844: 
 1845: =item C<max_bars> (integer, default C<0>)
 1846: 
 1847: When non-zero, only the first C<max_bars> entries (after any sort) are
 1848: rendered; the remaining entries are collapsed into a single C<Other> bar
 1849: whose value equals their sum.  Zero means no limit.
 1850: 
 1851: =item C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>)
 1852: 
 1853: Either a CSS colour value (e.g. C<'steelblue'>, C<'#4e79a7'>) applied to all
 1854: bars, or the special token C<'categorical'>, which colours each bar
 1855: distinctively using D3's Tableau-10 palette.
 1856: 
 1857: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 1858: ignored and the default used.
 1859: 
 1860: =item C<show_values> (bool, default C<0>)
 1861: 
 1862: When true, the numeric value of each bar is printed above vertical bars or
 1863: to the right of horizontal bars.
 1864: 
 1865: =item C<animated> (bool, default C<0>)
 1866: 
 1867: When true, bars grow from the baseline on page load: vertical bars rise from
 1868: the bottom; horizontal bars extend from the left.  An 800 ms D3 transition
 1869: with a per-bar stagger up to 100 ms is used.  The animation is suppressed
 1870: when C<prefers-reduced-motion> is set in the viewer's OS.
 1871: 
 1872: =item C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>)
 1873: 
 1874: Label shown in the hover tooltip before the numeric value.  The value is
 1875: JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1876: 
 1877: =item C<x_label> (string, default C<''>)
 1878: 
 1879: When non-empty, a text label is rendered below the bottom axis.  The value
 1880: is JavaScript-escaped before being embedded in the page.
 1881: 
 1882: =back
 1883: 
 1884: =head3 Errors
 1885: 
 1886: =over 4
 1887: 
 1888: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an
 1889: ARRAY reference.
 1890: 
 1891: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be an array reference> when an element
 1892: is not an array reference.
 1893: 
 1894: =item * Throws C<Each data point must have at least 2 elements> when an
 1895: element has fewer than 2 items.
 1896: 
 1897: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a C<$value> is not a number.
 1898: 
 1899: =item * Throws C<orientation must be 'vertical' or 'horizontal'> on an
 1900: invalid orientation value.
 1901: 
 1902: =item * Throws C<sort_bars must be 'value', 'label', or 'none'> on an
 1903: invalid sort_bars value.
 1904: 
 1905: =back
 1906: 
 1907: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 1908: 
 1909: =head4 Input
 1910: 
 1911:     {
 1912:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 1913:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1 },
 1914: 	orientation => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'vertical', 'horizontal' ], optional => 1 },
 1915: 	sort_bars => { type => 'string', memberof => [ 'value', 'label', 'none' ], optional => 1 }
 1916:     }
 1917: 
 1918:     Each element of C<$data>: C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 1919:     undef C<$value> silently skipped; negative C<$value> becomes positive.
 1920: 
 1921: =head4 Output
 1922: 
 1923:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'bar_chart', html => Str }
 1924:     html is a Perl character string (UTF-8 flag set, or pure ASCII).
 1925: 
 1926: =cut

 2263: 
 2264: =head2 render_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2265: 
 2266:     $html = $chart->render_line_chart_with_tooltips($data);
 2267: 
 2268: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2269: Accepts the following arguments:
 2270: 
 2271: =over 4
 2272: 
 2273: =item * C<$data> - An array reference containing data points. Each data point should
 2274: be an array reference with two elements: the label (string) and the value (numeric).
 2275: 
 2276: =back
 2277: 
 2278: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2279: The JavaScript tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> (with a backslash) rather than
 2280: C<< </b> >> to satisfy html-tidy's requirement that C<< </ >> followed by a
 2281: letter not appear literally inside C<< <script> >> blocks.
 2282: 
 2283: =head3 Errors
 2284: 
 2285: =over 4
 2286: 
 2287: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of arrays> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 2288: 
 2289: =back
 2290: 
 2291: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2292: 
 2293: =head4 Input
 2294: 
 2295:     {
 2296:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2297:     }
 2298: 
 2299:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]>; passing C<undef> or a
 2300:     non-arrayref dies.
 2301: 
 2302: =head4 Output
 2303: 
 2304:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; mouseover tooltip reveals label and value.
 2305:            Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> not C<< </b> >>.
 2306: 
 2307: =cut
 2308:

 2357: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
 2358: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;

 2371:

 2424: =head2 render_line_chart_snippet
 2425: 
 2426:     my $fragment = $chart->render_line_chart_snippet($data);
 2427:     my $fragment = $chart->render_line_chart_snippet($data, \%opts);
 2428:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2429:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 2430: 
 2431: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a line chart with mouseover tooltips.
 2432: Unlike C<render_line_chart_with_tooltips>, this method returns a fragment with
 2433: no C<<!DOCTYPE>>, C<<html>>, C<<head>>, or C<<body>> wrapper, suitable for
 2434: splicing directly into a Mojolicious TT (or any other) layout.
 2435: 
 2436: The caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page C<<head>>, e.g.:
 2437: 
 2438:     <script src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
 2439: 
 2440: =head3 Arguments
 2441: 
 2442: =over 4
 2443: 
 2444: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points. Each point is an array
 2445: reference with two required elements - the label (string) and the value
 2446: (numeric) - and an optional third element: a hash reference of extra key/value
 2447: pairs to display in the tooltip after the label and value rows.
 2448: 
 2449:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 2450:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 2451: 
 2452: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference of rendering options:
 2453: 
 2454: =over 4
 2455: 
 2456: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - Override the C<id> attribute of
 2457: the C<<svg>> element.  Use this when embedding multiple charts on the same page.
 2458: 
 2459: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default C<0>) - If true, emit
 2460: C<viewBox="0 0 W H" width="100%" height="auto"> instead of fixed pixel
 2461: dimensions, so the chart scales with its container.  Falls back to
 2462: C<< $self->{responsive} >> when not set per call.
 2463: 
 2464: =back
 2465: 
 2466: =back
 2467: 
 2468: =head3 Return value
 2469: 
 2470: A hash reference with:
 2471: 
 2472: =over 4
 2473: 
 2474: =item * C<svg_id> - The C<id> attribute used on the C<<svg>> element.
 2475: 
 2476: =item * C<html> - The embeddable fragment string (Perl character string).
 2477: 
 2478: =back
 2479: 
 2480: =head3 Errors
 2481: 
 2482: =over 4
 2483: 
 2484: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an
 2485: ARRAY reference.
 2486: 
 2487: =back
 2488: 
 2489: =head3 Side effects
 2490: 
 2491: None.  The method is read-only.
 2492: 
 2493: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2494: 
 2495:     {
 2496:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2497:         opts => {
 2498:             type     => 'hashref',
 2499:             optional => 1,
 2500:             keys     => {
 2501:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 2502:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 2503:             },
 2504:         },
 2505:     }
 2506: 
 2507: =cut
 2508: 
 2509: sub render_line_chart_snippet
 2510: {
 2511: 	my $self = shift;
 2512: 	my $raw  = Params::Get::get_params('data', \@_) // {};
 2513: 	die 'Data must be an array of arrays' unless ref($raw->{data}) eq 'ARRAY';
 2514: 	Params::Validate::Strict::validate_strict({
 2515: 		args   => $raw,
 2516: 		schema => { data => { type => 'arrayref', error_msg => 'Data must be an array of arrays' } },
 2517: 		unknown_parameter_handler => 'ignore',
 2518: 	});
 2519: 	my $data = $raw->{data};
 2520: 	my $opts = {%$raw}; delete $opts->{data};
 2521: 
 2522: 	my $json_data = _script_json([
 2523: 		map {
 2524: 			my $point = { label => $_->[0], value => $_->[1] };
 2525: 			$point->{extra} = $_->[2] if ref($_->[2]) eq 'HASH';
 2526: 			$point
 2527: 		} @$data
 2528: 	]);
 2529: 
 2530: 	my $svg_id  = _chart_id($opts->{id}) // 'chart';

 2627: =head2 render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet
 2628: 
 2629:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data);
 2630:     my $fragment = $chart->render_zoomable_line_chart_snippet($data, { animated => 1 });
 2631:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 2632:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + button + svg + script)
 2633: 
 2634: Like C<render_line_chart_snippet>, but adds brush-to-zoom: the user can drag
 2635: across a range of the x-axis to zoom into that region. A I<Reset zoom> button
 2636: (hidden until a zoom is active) returns the chart to its original extent.
 2637: Subsequent brushes on the zoomed view zoom in further; Reset always returns to
 2638: the full dataset.
 2639: 
 2640: The caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page C<<head>>.
 2641: 
 2642: Accepts the same arguments as C<render_line_chart_snippet>: an array reference
 2643: of data points, each C<[$x, $y]> or C<[$x, $y, \%extra]>, plus an optional
 2644: second argument C<$opts> (hashref).
 2645: 
 2646: =head3 Options
 2647: 
 2648: =over 4
 2649: 
 2650: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>) - when true, the initial page load
 2651: animates the line drawing left-to-right via the C<stroke-dashoffset> technique
 2652: (1200 ms, C<d3.easeLinear>), then fades in data-point circles after the line
 2653: finishes (300 ms after a 1200 ms delay).  Respects
 2654: C<prefers-reduced-motion>: when the user has requested reduced motion the line
 2655: is drawn immediately at full opacity.  Subsequent zoom and reset redraws are
 2656: never animated regardless of this flag.
 2657: 
 2658: =back
 2659: 
 2660: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2661: 
 2662: =head4 Input
 2663: 
 2664:     {
 2665:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2666:         opts => { type => 'hashref', optional => 1, default => {} },
 2667:     }
 2668: 
 2669:     Each element of C<$data> is C<[ Str, Num ]> or C<[ Str, Num, HashRef ]>;
 2670:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 2671:     Recognised C<opts> key: C<animated> (boolean, default C<0>).
 2672: 
 2673: =head4 Output
 2674: 
 2675:     HashRef -- C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'chart', html =E<gt> Str }>;
 2676:                embeddable fragment; no DOCTYPE, no page shell, no D3 CDN tag.
 2677: 
 2678: =head3 Errors
 2679: 
 2680: Dies with I<Data must be an array of arrays> if C<$data> is not an arrayref.
 2681: 
 2682: =cut

 2920: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2921: 
 2922:     $html = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips($data);
 2923: 
 2924: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a chart of many lines with mouseover tooltips.
 2925: 
 2926: Accepts the following arguments:
 2927: 
 2928: =over 4
 2929: 
 2930: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of series hashes. Each element is a hashref
 2931: with a C<name> key (string) and a C<data> key (array reference of C<< {label, value} >>
 2932: hashrefs).
 2933: 
 2934:     [
 2935:         { name => 'Series A', data => [{ label => 'Jan', value => 100 }, ...] },
 2936:         ...
 2937:     ]
 2938: 
 2939: =back
 2940: 
 2941: Returns a string containing the HTML and JavaScript code for the chart.
 2942: Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> rather than C<< </b> >> for html-tidy compliance.
 2943: 
 2944: =head3 Errors
 2945: 
 2946: =over 4
 2947: 
 2948: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 2949: 
 2950: =back
 2951: 
 2952: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 2953: 
 2954: =head4 Input
 2955: 
 2956:     {
 2957:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 2958:     }
 2959: 
 2960:     Each element of C<$data> is a hashref with keys C<name> (string) and
 2961:     C<data> (arrayref of hashrefs with C<label> and C<value> keys);
 2962:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 2963: 
 2964: =head4 Output
 2965: 
 2966:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; one coloured line per series with mouseover tooltips.
 2967: 
 2968: =cut

 2970: sub render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips
 2971: {
 2972: 	my $self = shift;

 3092: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_with_animated_tooltips
 3093: 
 3094:     $html = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_with_animated_tooltips($data);
 3095: 
 3096: Generates HTML and JavaScript code to render a chart of many lines with animated mouseover tooltips.
 3097: 
 3098: Accepts the following arguments:
 3099: 
 3100: =over 4
 3101: 
 3102: =item * C<$data> - Same format as C<render_multi_series_line_chart_with_tooltips>:
 3103: an array reference of C<< { name, data } >> series hashes.
 3104: 
 3105: =back
 3106: 
 3107: Returns a string containing the complete HTML5 document.
 3108: The tooltip appears with a CSS C<translateY> slide-in animation.
 3109: Tooltip strings use C<< <\/b> >> for html-tidy compliance.
 3110: 
 3111: =head3 Errors
 3112: 
 3113: =over 4
 3114: 
 3115: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 3116: 
 3117: =back
 3118: 
 3119: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 3120: 
 3121: =head4 Input
 3122: 
 3123:     {
 3124:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 3125:     }
 3126: 
 3127:     Each element of C<$data> is a hashref with keys C<name> (string) and
 3128:     C<data> (arrayref of hashrefs with C<label> and C<value> keys);
 3129:     passing C<undef> or a non-arrayref dies.
 3130: 
 3131: =head4 Output
 3132: 
 3133:     Str -- complete HTML5 document; animated tooltip uses CSS translateY transition.
 3134: 
 3135: =cut
 3136: 
 3137: sub render_multi_series_line_chart_with_animated_tooltips
 3138: {
 3139: 	my $self = shift;
 3140: 	my $raw  = Params::Get::get_params('data', \@_) // {};
 3141: 	die 'Data must be an array of hashes' unless ref($raw->{data}) eq 'ARRAY';
 3142: 	Params::Validate::Strict::validate_strict({
 3143: 		args   => $raw,
 3144: 		schema => { data => { type => 'arrayref', error_msg => 'Data must be an array of hashes' } },
 3145: 		unknown_parameter_handler => 'ignore',
 3146: 	});
 3147: 	my $data = $raw->{data};
 3148: 
 3149: 	# Generate JSON for data
 3150: 	my $json_data = _script_json($data);
 3151: 
 3152: 	# Generate HTML and D3.js code
 3153: 	my $html = $self->_preamble();
 3154: 	$html .= <<"HTML";
 3155: <head>
 3156:     <meta charset="UTF-8">
 3157:     <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
 3158:     <title>@{[ $self->_title_html() ]}</title>
 3159:     <script src="https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"></script>
 3160:     <style>
 3161: 	.tooltip {
 3162: 	    position: absolute;
 3163: 	    background-color: white;
 3164: 	    border: 1px solid #ccc;
 3165: 	    padding: 5px;
 3166: 	    font-size: 12px;
 3167: 	    pointer-events: none;
 3168: 	    opacity: 0;
 3169: 	    transform: translateY(-10px);
 3170: 	    transition: opacity 0.2s ease-in-out, transform 0.2s ease-in-out;
 3171: 	}
 3172:     </style>
 3173: </head>
 3174: <body>
 3175:     <h1 style="text-align: center;">@{[ $self->_title_html() ]}</h1>
 3176:     <svg id="chart" ${\ $self->_svg_attrs() }></svg>
 3177:     <div class="tooltip" id="tooltip"></div>
 3178:     <script>
 3179: 	const data = $json_data;
 3180: 
 3181: 	const svg = d3.select("#chart");
 3182: 	const tooltip = d3.select("#tooltip");
 3183: 	const margin = { top: 20, right: 30, bottom: 40, left: 40 };
 3184: 	const width = $self->{width} - margin.left - margin.right;
 3185: 	const height = $self->{height} - margin.top - margin.bottom;
 3186: 
 3187: 	const chart = svg.append("g")
 3188: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(\${margin.left},\${margin.top})`);
 3189: 
 3190: 	// Extract all labels and flatten them into a unique array
 3191: 	const allLabels = Array.from(new Set(data.flatMap(series => series.data.map(d => d.label))));
 3192: 
 3193: 	const x = d3.scalePoint()
 3194: 	    .domain(allLabels)
 3195: 	    .range([0, width]);
 3196: 
 3197: 	const y = d3.scaleLinear()
 3198: 	    .domain([0, d3.max(data.flatMap(series => series.data.map(d => d.value)))])
 3199: 	    .nice()
 3200: 	    .range([height, 0]);
 3201: 
 3202: 	// Define color scale for series
 3203: 	const color = d3.scaleOrdinal($CATEGORY10_JS);
 3204: 
 3205: 	// Add axes
 3206: 	chart.append("g")
 3207: 	    .call(d3.axisLeft(y));
 3208: 
 3209: 	chart.append("g")
 3210: 	    .attr("transform", `translate(0,\${height})`)
 3211: 	    .call(d3.axisBottom(x))
 3212: 	    .selectAll("text")
 3213: 	    .attr("transform", "rotate(-45)")
 3214: 	    .style("text-anchor", "end");
 3215: 
 3216: 	// Draw lines for each series
 3217: 	data.forEach((series, i) => {
 3218: 	    const line = d3.line()
 3219: 		.x(d => x(d.label))
 3220: 		.y(d => y(d.value));
 3221: 
 3222: 	    // Add line
 3223: 	    chart.append("path")
 3224: 		.datum(series.data)
 3225: 		.attr("fill", "none")
 3226: 		.attr("stroke", color(i))
 3227: 		.attr("stroke-width", 2)
 3228: 		.attr("d", line);
 3229: 
 3230: 	    // Add points and tooltips
 3231: 	    chart.selectAll(\`circle.series-\${i}\`)
 3232: 		.data(series.data)
 3233: 		.join("circle")
 3234: 		.attr("class", \`series-\${i}\`)
 3235: 		.attr("cx", d => x(d.label))
 3236: 		.attr("cy", d => y(d.value))
 3237: 		.attr("r", 4)
 3238: 		.attr("fill", color(i))
 3239: 		.on("mouseover", (event, d) => {
 3240: 		    tooltip.style("opacity", 1)
 3241: 			   .style("transform", "translateY(0)")
 3242: 			   .html(\`Series: <b>\${series.name}<\\/b><br>Label: <b>\${d.label}<\\/b><br>Value: <b>\${d.value}<\\/b>\`)
 3243: 			   .style("left", (event.pageX + 10) + "px")
 3244: 			   .style("top", (event.pageY - 30) + "px");
 3245: 		})
 3246: 		.on("mousemove", (event) => {
 3247: 		    tooltip.style("left", (event.pageX + 10) + "px")
 3248: 			   .style("top", (event.pageY - 30) + "px");
 3249: 		})
 3250: 		.on("mouseout", () => {
 3251: 		    tooltip.style("opacity", 0)
 3252: 			   .style("transform", "translateY(-10px)");
 3253: 		});
 3254: 	});
 3255:     </script>
 3256: </body>
 3257: </html>
 3258: HTML
 3259: 
 3260:     return $html;

  Mutants (Total: 2, Killed: 2, Survived: 0)
 3261: }

 3684: =head2 render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet
 3685: 
 3686:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series);
 3687:     my $fragment = $chart->render_multi_series_line_chart_snippet(\@series, \%opts);
 3688:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 3689:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 3690: 
 3691: Generates an embeddable multi-series line chart fragment.  Unlike
 3692: C<render_multi_series_line_chart_with_interactive_legends>, this method returns
 3693: a bare fragment with no C<< <html> >>, C<< <head> >>, or C<< <body> >> wrapper;
 3694: D3.js must be loaded by the host page.
 3695: 
 3696: =head3 Arguments
 3697: 
 3698: =over 4
 3699: 
 3700: =item * C<\@series> - An array reference of series.  Each series is a hash
 3701: reference with a C<name> (string) and a C<data> array reference of
 3702: C<< { label => Str, value => Num } >> points:
 3703: 
 3704:     [
 3705:         { name => 'North', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value => 12 }, ... ] },
 3706:         { name => 'South', data => [ { label => 'Jan', value =>  8 }, ... ] },
 3707:     ]
 3708: 
 3709: Series need not share labels: the X domain is the union of every series' labels,
 3710: in first-seen order.  One line and one set of dots is drawn per series, and a
 3711: clickable legend toggles each series on and off.
 3712: 
 3713: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 3714: 
 3715: =over 4
 3716: 
 3717: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 3718: scheme (for example C<'category10'>, C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, C<'paired'>).
 3719: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 3720: the colours of one.
 3721: 
 3722: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown below the X axis.
 3723: 
 3724: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label shown beside the Y axis.
 3725: 
 3726: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true each line draws itself left
 3727: to right by animating its stroke dash offset.  Respects
 3728: C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 3729: 
 3730: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 3731: 
 3732: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 3733: 
 3734: =back
 3735: 
 3736: =back
 3737: 
 3738: Returns C<< { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str } >>.
 3739: 
 3740: =head3 Errors
 3741: 
 3742: =over 4
 3743: 
 3744: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of series> when C<\@series> is not an
 3745: ARRAY reference.
 3746: 
 3747: =item * Throws C<Each series must be a hashref with name and data> when a
 3748: series is not a hashref, has no C<name>, or has a C<data> that is not an ARRAY
 3749: reference.
 3750: 
 3751: =back
 3752: 
 3753: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 3754: 
 3755: =head4 Input
 3756: 
 3757:     {
 3758:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 3759:         opts => {
 3760:             type     => 'hashref',
 3761:             optional => 1,
 3762:             keys     => {
 3763:                 scheme     => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 3764:                 x_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3765:                 y_label    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 3766:                 animated   => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 3767:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 3768:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'chart' },
 3769:             },
 3770:         },
 3771:     }
 3772: 
 3773: =head4 Output
 3774: 
 3775:     HashRef -- { svg_id => 'chart', html => Str }
 3776: 
 3777: =cut

 3962: =head2 render_scatter_chart_snippet
 3963: 
 3964:     my $fragment = $chart->render_scatter_chart_snippet($data);
 3965:     my $fragment = $chart->render_scatter_chart_snippet($data, \%opts);
 3966:     # $fragment->{svg_id} - the id attribute of the <svg> element
 3967:     # $fragment->{html}   - embeddable HTML fragment (style + svg + script)
 3968: 
 3969: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a scatter plot with mouseover
 3970: tooltips.  Returns a fragment with no C<<!DOCTYPE>>, C<<html>>, C<<head>>, or
 3971: C<<body>> wrapper; the caller is responsible for loading D3 in the page.
 3972: 
 3973: =head3 Arguments
 3974: 
 3975: =over 4
 3976: 
 3977: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of data points.  Each point is an array
 3978: reference with two required numeric elements (x, y), an optional third hash
 3979: reference of extra key/value pairs shown as extra tooltip rows, and an optional
 3980: fourth hash reference carrying the C<series> (colour group) and C<size> (bubble
 3981: radius) dimensions:
 3982: 
 3983:     [$x, $y]          # basic point
 3984:     [$x, $y, \%row]   # point with extra tooltip data
 3985:     [$x, $y, \%row, { series => 'North', size => 42 }]   # colour and size
 3986: 
 3987: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 3988: 
 3989: =over 4
 3990: 
 3991: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'scatter_chart'>) - C<id> of the C<<svg>> element.
 3992: 
 3993: =item * C<color> (CSS colour or C<'categorical'>, default C<'steelblue'>) -
 3994: Fill colour for the data points.  C<'categorical'> uses the Tableau-10 palette.
 3995: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 3996: ignored and the default used.
 3997: 
 3998: =item * C<x_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the X axis.
 3999: 
 4000: =item * C<y_label> (string, default C<''>) - Label for the Y axis.
 4001: 
 4002: =item * C<value_label> (string, default C<'Value'>) - Prefix for the tooltip value row.
 4003: 
 4004: =item * C<colour_by_series> (boolean, default 0) - Colour each point by its
 4005: C<series> value instead of by point index, and draw a legend listing the series
 4006: to the right of the plot.  Ignored for points with no C<series> value.
 4007: 
 4008: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour
 4009: scheme used when C<colour_by_series> is set, for example C<'category10'>,
 4010: C<'set2'>, C<'set3'>, or C<'paired'>.
 4011: C<categorical_schemes()> lists the names, and C<palette()> hands a Perl caller
 4012: the colours of one.
 4013: 
 4014: =item * C<series_col> (string, default C<'Series'>) - Row label for the series
 4015: value in the tooltip.
 4016: 
 4017: =item * C<size_col> (string, default C<'Size'>) - Row label for the size value
 4018: in the tooltip.
 4019: 
 4020: =item * C<radius_range> (arrayref of two numbers, default C<[3, 15]>) - Pixel
 4021: radii mapped from the smallest and largest observed C<size> value.  The mapping
 4022: is by area (C<d3.scaleSqrt>), so a point twice as large is drawn with four
 4023: times the area.  A point with no C<size> value is drawn at the lower radius.
 4024: 
 4025: =item * C<animated> (boolean, default 0) - If true, circles fade in from
 4026: opacity 0 on page load (400 ms).  Respects C<prefers-reduced-motion>.
 4027: 
 4028: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, default 0) - See C<render_bar_chart_snippet>.
 4029: 
 4030: =back
 4031: 
 4032: =back
 4033:

 4350: =head2 render_table_snippet
 4351: 
 4352:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data);
 4353:     my $fragment = $chart->render_table_snippet($data, \%opts);
 4354:     # $fragment->{table_id} - the id attribute of the <table> element
 4355:     # $fragment->{html}     - embeddable HTML fragment (style + table + script)
 4356: 
 4357: Generates an embeddable HTML fragment for a sortable, filterable data table.
 4358: Returns a fragment with no page-shell wrapper; the caller loads D3 if needed.
 4359: 
 4360: =head3 Arguments
 4361: 
 4362: =over 4
 4363: 
 4364: =item * C<$data> - An array reference of row array references.  The first row
 4365: is treated as the header row.  Every subsequent row must have the same number
 4366: of columns as the header.
 4367: 
 4368:     [ ['Name', 'Value', 'Category'],   # header
 4369:       ['Alpha',   300,  'A'],
 4370:       ['Beta',    150,  'B'],
 4371:     ]
 4372: 
 4373: =item * C<\%opts> - Optional hash reference:
 4374: 
 4375: =over 4
 4376: 
 4377: =item * C<id> (string, default C<'data_table'>) - C<id> of the C<<table>> element.
 4378: 
 4379: =item * C<sortable> (boolean, default 1) - If true, clicking a column header
 4380: sorts the table by that column (toggle ascending/descending).
 4381: 
 4382: =item * C<caption> (string, default C<''>) - Optional C<<caption>> element text.
 4383: 
 4384: =back
 4385: 
 4386: =back
 4387: 
 4388: =head3 Return value
 4389: 
 4390: A hash reference with C<table_id> (string) and C<html> (Perl character string).
 4391: Note: returns C<table_id>, not C<svg_id>, because this method renders an HTML
 4392: table rather than an SVG chart.
 4393: 
 4394: =head3 Errors
 4395: 
 4396: =over 4
 4397: 
 4398: =item * Dies with C<'Data must be an array of arrays'> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY ref.
 4399: 
 4400: =item * Dies with C<'Data must have at least one row (header row)'> when C<$data> is empty.
 4401: 
 4402: =item * Dies with C<'Each row must be an array reference'> when a row is not an ARRAY ref.
 4403: 
 4404: =back
 4405: 
 4406: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4407: 
 4408:     {
 4409:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4410:         opts => {
 4411:             type     => 'hashref',
 4412:             optional => 1,
 4413:             keys     => {
 4414:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'data_table' },
 4415:                 sortable => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 4416:                 caption  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 4417:             },
 4418:         },
 4419:     }
 4420: 
 4421: =cut
 4422:

 4545: =head2 render_stacked_bar_snippet
 4546: 
 4547:     my $result = $chart->render_stacked_bar_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4548: 
 4549: Generates an embeddable stacked bar chart (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 4550: 
 4551: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with three keys named by C<opts>:
 4552: C<cat_col> (the X-axis grouping), C<seg_col> (the stacking segment), and
 4553: C<val_col> (the numeric measure).  Multiple rows sharing the same
 4554: cat+seg combination are summed.
 4555: 
 4556: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4557: 
 4558: =over 4
 4559: 
 4560: =item * C<cat_col> (string, default C<'cat'>) - row key for the X-axis category.
 4561: 
 4562: =item * C<seg_col> (string, default C<'seg'>) - row key for the stacking segment.
 4563: 
 4564: =item * C<val_col> (string, default C<'val'>) - row key for the numeric value.
 4565: 
 4566: =item * C<orient> (string C<'v'> or C<'h'>, default C<'v'>) - bar orientation.
 4567: 
 4568: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 4569: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 4570: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 4571: Perl.
 4572: 
 4573: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - axis label shown below the category axis.
 4574: 
 4575: =item * C<sort> (string, optional) - sort categories: C<'value'> (desc total)
 4576: or C<'label'> (alpha).
 4577: 
 4578: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4579: 
 4580: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4581: 
 4582: =back
 4583: 
 4584: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'stacked_bar', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4585: 
 4586: =head3 Errors
 4587: 
 4588: =over 4
 4589: 
 4590: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4591: 
 4592: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4593: 
 4594: =item * Throws C<Value must be numeric> when a value field is not a number.
 4595:

 4838: 
 4839: =head2 render_combo_snippet
 4840: 
 4841:     my $result = $chart->render_combo_snippet(\@rows, \%opts);
 4842: 
 4843: Generates an embeddable combo chart: bars for one measure on the left Y
 4844: axis, a line for another measure on the right Y axis, sharing the same
 4845: X (category) axis.
 4846: 
 4847: Each element of C<\@rows> is a hashref with at least three keys named by
 4848: C<opts>: C<x_col> (X-axis label), C<bar_col> (bar measure), and
 4849: C<line_col> (line measure).
 4850: 
 4851: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 4852: 
 4853: =over 4
 4854: 
 4855: =item * C<x_col> (string, default C<'x'>) - row key for the X-axis label.
 4856: 
 4857: =item * C<bar_col> (string, default C<'bar_val'>) - row key for the bar measure.
 4858: 
 4859: =item * C<line_col> (string, default C<'line_val'>) - row key for the line measure.
 4860: 
 4861: =item * C<bar_label> (string, default C<'Bar'>) - legend/axis label for bars.
 4862: 
 4863: =item * C<line_label> (string, default C<'Line'>) - legend/axis label for line.
 4864: 
 4865: =item * C<bar_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill.
 4866: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 4867: ignored and the default used.
 4868: 
 4869: =item * C<line_color> (string, optional CSS colour, default C<'#e15759'>) - line stroke.
 4870: Checked as C<bar_color> is.
 4871: 
 4872: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 4873: 
 4874: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 4875: 
 4876: =back
 4877: 
 4878: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'combo_chart', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 4879: 
 4880: =head3 Errors
 4881: 
 4882: =over 4
 4883: 
 4884: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes> when C<$data> is not an ARRAY reference.
 4885: 
 4886: =item * Throws C<Each data point must be a hash reference> when a row is not a hashref.
 4887: 
 4888: =item * Throws C<Bar value must be numeric> when a bar value field is not a number.
 4889: 
 4890: =item * Throws C<Line value must be numeric> when a line value field is not a number.
 4891: 
 4892: =back
 4893: 
 4894: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 4895: 
 4896: =head4 Input
 4897: 
 4898:     {
 4899:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 4900:         opts => {
 4901:             type     => 'hashref',
 4902:             optional => 1,
 4903:             keys     => {
 4904:                 x_col      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'x' },
 4905:                 bar_col    => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'bar_val' },
 4906:                 line_col   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'line_val' },
 4907:                 bar_label  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'Bar' },
 4908:                 line_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'Line' },
 4909:                 bar_color  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 4910:                 line_color => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '#e15759' },
 4911:                 id         => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'combo_chart' },
 4912:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 4913:             },
 4914:         },
 4915:     }
 4916: 
 4917: =head4 Output
 4918: 
 4919:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 4920: 
 4921: =cut

 5093: =head2 render_treemap_snippet
 5094: 
 5095:     my $result = $chart->render_treemap_snippet(\@rows_or_tree, \%opts);
 5096: 
 5097: Generates an embeddable treemap (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 5098: Leaf area is proportional to the numeric measure.
 5099: 
 5100: Accepts two data formats:
 5101: 
 5102: =over 4
 5103: 
 5104: =item * B<Flat path> (arrayref of hashrefs): each row must have a C<path>
 5105: key (e.g. C<'Root/A/X'>) and a C<value> key.  The module converts this
 5106: to a D3 stratify hierarchy in JS.  Override the path separator with
 5107: C<opts-E<gt>{path_sep}>.
 5108: 
 5109: =item * B<Nested tree> (hashref): a pre-built D3-compatible hierarchy
 5110: with C<name>/C<children>/C<value> structure.  Passed directly to
 5111: C<d3.hierarchy()>.
 5112: 
 5113: =back
 5114: 
 5115: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5116: 
 5117: =over 4
 5118: 
 5119: =item * C<path_sep> (string, default C<'/'>)  - path separator (flat format only).
 5120: 
 5121: =item * C<scheme> (string, default C<'tableau10'>) - D3 categorical colour scheme.
 5122: Supported: C<tableau10>, C<category10>, C<set2>, C<set3>, C<paired> - the names
 5123: C<categorical_schemes()> returns, whose colours C<palette()> makes available to
 5124: Perl.
 5125: 
 5126: =item * C<label> (boolean, default C<1>) - show node labels inside cells.
 5127: 
 5128: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5129: 
 5130: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5131: 
 5132: =back
 5133: 
 5134: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'treemap', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5135: 
 5136: =head3 Errors
 5137: 
 5138: =over 4
 5139: 
 5140: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of hashes or a hash tree>
 5141: when C<$data> is neither an ARRAY nor a HASH reference.
 5142: 
 5143: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must be a hash reference> when a flat row is not a hashref.
 5144: 
 5145: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a 'path' key> when a flat row lacks C<path>.
 5146: 
 5147: =item * Throws C<Each flat row must have a numeric 'value' key> when a value is not numeric.
 5148: 
 5149: =back
 5150: 
 5151: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5152: 
 5153: =head4 Input
 5154: 
 5155:     {
 5156:         data => { type => 'arrayref | hashref' },
 5157:         opts => {
 5158:             type     => 'hashref',
 5159:             optional => 1,
 5160:             keys     => {
 5161:                 path_sep => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '/' },
 5162:                 scheme   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'tableau10' },
 5163:                 label    => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 1 },
 5164:                 id       => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'treemap' },
 5165:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5166:             },
 5167:         },
 5168:     }
 5169: 
 5170: =head4 Output
 5171: 
 5172:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5173: 
 5174: =cut
 5175:

 5329: 
 5330: =head2 render_histogram_snippet
 5331: 
 5332:     my $result = $chart->render_histogram_snippet(\@values, \%opts);
 5333: 
 5334: Generates an embeddable frequency histogram (no page shell, no CDN tag).
 5335: C<\@values> is an arrayref of numeric scalars (already extracted and
 5336: coerced by the caller).
 5337: 
 5338: Accepted C<\%opts> keys:
 5339: 
 5340: =over 4
 5341: 
 5342: =item * C<bins> (integer or C<undef>, default C<20>) - number of bins.
 5343: Pass C<undef> for automatic bin count using Sturges' rule:
 5344: C<ceil(log2(n) + 1)>.  Ignored when C<binned> is set.
 5345: 
 5346: =item * C<binned> (boolean, default 0) - treat C<$data> as pre-computed bins
 5347: rather than as raw values.  Each element is a hashref with a C<count> and
 5348: either a C<label> or a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair from which the label C<[lo,hi)>
 5349: is derived:
 5350: 
 5351:     { label => '[0,10)', count => 3 }
 5352:     { lo => 10, hi => 20, count => 5 }
 5353: 
 5354: The caller owns the binning, so C<d3.bin()> is never called: one bar is drawn
 5355: per entry, in the order supplied, and the supplied counts are authoritative.
 5356: 
 5357: The bin labels are drawn rotated.  The plot gives up as much room as they need,
 5358: up to half the chart's height and a quarter of its width, so that they and
 5359: C<x_label> do not overlap and stay inside the chart.
 5360: 
 5361: =item * C<scheme> (string, optional) - D3 categorical colour scheme (for example
 5362: C<'tableau10'> or C<'category10'>) whose first colour is used as the bar fill
 5363: when C<color> is not given.
 5364: That colour is C<colour_at($scheme, 0)>, so a caller shading the same data by
 5365: hand starts from the same colour the bars do.
 5366: 
 5367: =item * C<x_label> (string, optional) - label shown below the X axis.
 5368: 
 5369: =item * C<color> (string, default C<'steelblue'>) - bar fill colour.
 5370: A value holding anything a CSS colour cannot, such as a quote or C<E<lt>>, is
 5371: ignored and the default used.
 5372: 
 5373: =item * C<id> (string, optional) - override the default C<svg_id> value.
 5374: 
 5375: =item * C<responsive> (boolean, optional) - SVG responsive sizing override.
 5376: 
 5377: =back
 5378: 
 5379: Returns C<{ svg_id =E<gt> 'histogram', html =E<gt> Str }>.
 5380: 
 5381: =head3 Errors
 5382: 
 5383: =over 4
 5384: 
 5385: =item * Throws C<Data must be an array of numeric values> when C<$data> is
 5386: not an ARRAY reference.
 5387: 
 5388: =item * Throws C<Each value must be numeric> when any element is non-numeric.
 5389: 
 5390: =item * Throws C<bins must be a positive integer> when C<bins> is defined
 5391: but not a positive integer.
 5392: 
 5393: =item * Throws C<Data must contain at least one bin> when C<binned> is set and
 5394: C<$data> holds no bins.
 5395: 
 5396: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must be a hash reference> when a
 5397: pre-binned element is not a hashref.
 5398: 
 5399: =item * Throws C<Bin count must be numeric> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5400: missing or not numeric.
 5401: 
 5402: =item * Throws C<Bin count must not be negative> when a pre-binned C<count> is
 5403: negative.
 5404: 
 5405: =item * Throws C<Each pre-binned entry must have a label or numeric lo and hi>
 5406: when a pre-binned entry has neither a C<label> nor a numeric C<lo>/C<hi> pair.
 5407: 
 5408: =back
 5409: 
 5410: =head3 API SPECIFICATION
 5411: 
 5412: =head4 Input
 5413: 
 5414:     {
 5415:         data => { type => 'arrayref' },
 5416:         opts => {
 5417:             type     => 'hashref',
 5418:             optional => 1,
 5419:             keys     => {
 5420:                 bins    => { type => 'integer', optional => 1, default => 20 },
 5421:                 binned  => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1, default => 0 },
 5422:                 x_label => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => '' },
 5423:                 color   => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'steelblue' },
 5424:                 scheme  => { type => 'string',  optional => 1 },
 5425:                 id      => { type => 'string',  optional => 1, default => 'histogram' },
 5426:                 responsive => { type => 'boolean', optional => 1 },
 5427:             },
 5428:         },
 5429:     }
 5430: 
 5431: =head4 Output
 5432: 
 5433:     HashRef -- { svg_id => Str, html => Str }
 5434: 
 5435: =cut

 5950: 
 5951: It would help to have the render routine to return the head and body components separately.
 5952:
